parent = <scippnexus.base.Group object at 0x7f9fe0336840>
depends_on = DependsOn(parent='/entry/instrument/normalization_monitor', value='/entry/instrument/normalization_monitor/transformations/normalization_monitor_t0_r')

    def parse_depends_on_chain(
        parent: Field | Group, depends_on: DependsOn
    ) -> TransformationChain | None:
        """Follow a depends_on chain and return the transformations."""
        chain = TransformationChain(depends_on.parent, depends_on.value)
        # Use raw h5py objects to follow the chain because that avoids constructing
        # expensive intermediate snx.Group objects.
        file = parent.underlying.file
        visited = [depends_on.absolute_path()]
        try:
            while not depends_on.is_terminal:
                transform, base = _locate_depends_on_target(
                    file, depends_on, parent.definitions
                )
                depends_on = DependsOn(parent=base, value=transform.attrs['depends_on'])
                chain.transformations[transform.name] = transform[()]
                if depends_on.absolute_path() in visited:
                    raise ValueError(
                        'Circular depends_on chain detected: '
                        f'{[*visited, depends_on.absolute_path()]}'
                    )
                visited.append(depends_on.absolute_path())
        except KeyError as e:
>           warnings.warn(
                UserWarning(
                    f'depends_on chain {depends_on} references missing node {e}'.replace(
                        '\n', ''
                    )
                ),
                stacklevel=2,
            )

parent = <scippnexus.base.Group object at 0x7fb6183e3e90>
depends_on = DependsOn(parent='/entry/instrument/normalization_monitor', value='/entry/instrument/normalization_monitor/transformations/normalization_monitor_t0_r')

    def parse_depends_on_chain(
        parent: Field | Group, depends_on: DependsOn
    ) -> TransformationChain | None:
        """Follow a depends_on chain and return the transformations."""
        chain = TransformationChain(depends_on.parent, depends_on.value)
        # Use raw h5py objects to follow the chain because that avoids constructing
        # expensive intermediate snx.Group objects.
        file = parent.underlying.file
        visited = [depends_on.absolute_path()]
        try:
            while not depends_on.is_terminal:
                transform, base = _locate_depends_on_target(
                    file, depends_on, parent.definitions
                )
                depends_on = DependsOn(parent=base, value=transform.attrs['depends_on'])
                chain.transformations[transform.name] = transform[()]
                if depends_on.absolute_path() in visited:
                    raise ValueError(
                        'Circular depends_on chain detected: '
                        f'{[*visited, depends_on.absolute_path()]}'
                    )
                visited.append(depends_on.absolute_path())
        except KeyError as e:
>           warnings.warn(
                UserWarning(
                    f'depends_on chain {depends_on} references missing node {e}'.replace(
                        '\n', ''
                    )
                ),
                stacklevel=2,
            )

parent = <scippnexus.base.Group object at 0x7f64182cbd40>
depends_on = DependsOn(parent='/entry/instrument/normalization_monitor', value='/entry/instrument/normalization_monitor/transformations/normalization_monitor_t0_r')

    def parse_depends_on_chain(
        parent: Field | Group, depends_on: DependsOn
    ) -> TransformationChain | None:
        """Follow a depends_on chain and return the transformations."""
        chain = TransformationChain(depends_on.parent, depends_on.value)
        # Use raw h5py objects to follow the chain because that avoids constructing
        # expensive intermediate snx.Group objects.
        file = parent.underlying.file
        visited = [depends_on.absolute_path()]
        try:
            while not depends_on.is_terminal:
                transform, base = _locate_depends_on_target(
                    file, depends_on, parent.definitions
                )
                depends_on = DependsOn(parent=base, value=transform.attrs['depends_on'])
                chain.transformations[transform.name] = transform[()]
                if depends_on.absolute_path() in visited:
                    raise ValueError(
                        'Circular depends_on chain detected: '
                        f'{[*visited, depends_on.absolute_path()]}'
                    )
                visited.append(depends_on.absolute_path())
        except KeyError as e:
>           warnings.warn(
                UserWarning(
                    f'depends_on chain {depends_on} references missing node {e}'.replace(
                        '\n', ''
                    )
                ),
                stacklevel=2,
            )

depends_on = DependsOn(parent='/entry/instrument/normalization_monitor', value='/entry/instrument/normalization_monitor/transformations/normalization_monitor_t0_r')

    def parse_depends_on_chain(
        parent: Field | Group, depends_on: DependsOn
    ) -> TransformationChain | None:
        """Follow a depends_on chain and return the transformations."""
        chain = TransformationChain(depends_on.parent, depends_on.value)
        # Use raw h5py objects to follow the chain because that avoids constructing
        # expensive intermediate snx.Group objects.
        file = parent.underlying.file
        visited = [depends_on.absolute_path()]
        try:
            while not depends_on.is_terminal:
                transform, base = _locate_depends_on_target(
                    file, depends_on, parent.definitions
                )
                depends_on = DependsOn(parent=base, value=transform.attrs['depends_on'])
                chain.transformations[transform.name] = transform[()]
                if depends_on.absolute_path() in visited:
                    raise ValueError(
                        'Circular depends_on chain detected: '
                        f'{[*visited, depends_on.absolute_path()]}'
                    )
                visited.append(depends_on.absolute_path())
        except KeyError as e:
>           warnings.warn(

depends_on = DependsOn(parent='/entry/instrument/normalization_monitor', value='/entry/instrument/normalization_monitor/transformations/normalization_monitor_t0_r')

    def parse_depends_on_chain(
        parent: Field | Group, depends_on: DependsOn
    ) -> TransformationChain | None:
        """Follow a depends_on chain and return the transformations."""
        chain = TransformationChain(depends_on.parent, depends_on.value)
        # Use raw h5py objects to follow the chain because that avoids constructing
        # expensive intermediate snx.Group objects.
        file = parent.underlying.file
        visited = [depends_on.absolute_path()]
        try:
            while not depends_on.is_terminal:
                transform, base = _locate_depends_on_target(
                    file, depends_on, parent.definitions
                )
                depends_on = DependsOn(parent=base, value=transform.attrs['depends_on'])
                chain.transformations[transform.name] = transform[()]
                if depends_on.absolute_path() in visited:
                    raise ValueError(
                        'Circular depends_on chain detected: '
                        f'{[*visited, depends_on.absolute_path()]}'
                    )
                visited.append(depends_on.absolute_path())
        except KeyError as e:

parent = <scippnexus.base.Group object at 0x7f6b7c1d9130>
depends_on = DependsOn(parent='/entry/instrument/normalization_monitor', value='/entry/instrument/normalization_monitor/transformations/normalization_monitor_t0_r')

    def parse_depends_on_chain(
        parent: Field | Group, depends_on: DependsOn
    ) -> TransformationChain | None:
        """Follow a depends_on chain and return the transformations."""
        chain = TransformationChain(depends_on.parent, depends_on.value)
        # Use raw h5py objects to follow the chain because that avoids constructing
        # expensive intermediate snx.Group objects.
        file = parent.underlying.file
        visited = [depends_on.absolute_path()]
        try:
            while not depends_on.is_terminal:
                transform, base = _locate_depends_on_target(
                    file, depends_on, parent.definitions
                )
                depends_on = DependsOn(parent=base, value=transform.attrs['depends_on'])
                chain.transformations[transform.name] = transform[()]
                if depends_on.absolute_path() in visited:
                    raise ValueError(
                        'Circular depends_on chain detected: '
                        f'{[*visited, depends_on.absolute_path()]}'
                    )
                visited.append(depends_on.absolute_path())
        except KeyError as e:
>           warnings.warn(
                UserWarning(
                    f'depends_on chain {depends_on} references missing node {e}'.replace(
                        '\n', ''
                    )
                ),
                stacklevel=2,
            )

depends_on = DependsOn(parent='/entry/instrument/normalization_monitor', value='/entry/instrument/normalization_monitor/transformations/normalization_monitor_t0_r')

    def parse_depends_on_chain(
        parent: Field | Group, depends_on: DependsOn
    ) -> TransformationChain | None:
        """Follow a depends_on chain and return the transformations."""
        chain = TransformationChain(depends_on.parent, depends_on.value)
        # Use raw h5py objects to follow the chain because that avoids constructing
        # expensive intermediate snx.Group objects.
        file = parent.underlying.file
        visited = [depends_on.absolute_path()]
        try:
            while not depends_on.is_terminal:
                transform, base = _locate_depends_on_target(
                    file, depends_on, parent.definitions
                )
                depends_on = DependsOn(parent=base, value=transform.attrs['depends_on'])
                chain.transformations[transform.name] = transform[()]
                if depends_on.absolute_path() in visited:
                    raise ValueError(
                        'Circular depends_on chain detected: '
                        f'{[*visited, depends_on.absolute_path()]}'
                    )
                visited.append(depends_on.absolute_path())
        except KeyError as e:
>           warnings.warn(
                UserWarning(
                    f'depends_on chain {depends_on} references missing node {e}'.replace(
                        '\n', ''
                    )
                ),
                stacklevel=2,
            )

parent = <scippnexus.base.Group object at 0x7f66483120c0>
depends_on = DependsOn(parent='/entry/instrument/normalization_monitor', value='/entry/instrument/normalization_monitor/transformations/normalization_monitor_t0_r')

    def parse_depends_on_chain(
        parent: Field | Group, depends_on: DependsOn
    ) -> TransformationChain | None:
        """Follow a depends_on chain and return the transformations."""
        chain = TransformationChain(depends_on.parent, depends_on.value)
        # Use raw h5py objects to follow the chain because that avoids constructing
        # expensive intermediate snx.Group objects.
        file = parent.underlying.file
        visited = [depends_on.absolute_path()]
        try:
            while not depends_on.is_terminal:
                transform, base = _locate_depends_on_target(
                    file, depends_on, parent.definitions
                )
                depends_on = DependsOn(parent=base, value=transform.attrs['depends_on'])
                chain.transformations[transform.name] = transform[()]
                if depends_on.absolute_path() in visited:
                    raise ValueError(
                        'Circular depends_on chain detected: '
                        f'{[*visited, depends_on.absolute_path()]}'
                    )
                visited.append(depends_on.absolute_path())
        except KeyError as e:
>           warnings.warn(
                UserWarning(
                    f'depends_on chain {depends_on} references missing node {e}'.replace(
                        '\n', ''
                    )
                ),
                stacklevel=2,

parent = <scippnexus.base.Group object at 0x7f0f24292f90>
depends_on = DependsOn(parent='/entry/instrument/normalization_monitor', value='/entry/instrument/normalization_monitor/transformations/normalization_monitor_t0_r')

    def parse_depends_on_chain(
        parent: Field | Group, depends_on: DependsOn
    ) -> TransformationChain | None:
        """Follow a depends_on chain and return the transformations."""
        chain = TransformationChain(depends_on.parent, depends_on.value)
        # Use raw h5py objects to follow the chain because that avoids constructing
        # expensive intermediate snx.Group objects.
        file = parent.underlying.file
        visited = [depends_on.absolute_path()]
        try:
            while not depends_on.is_terminal:
                transform, base = _locate_depends_on_target(
                    file, depends_on, parent.definitions
                )
                depends_on = DependsOn(parent=base, value=transform.attrs['depends_on'])
                chain.transformations[transform.name] = transform[()]
                if depends_on.absolute_path() in visited:
                    raise ValueError(
                        'Circular depends_on chain detected: '
                        f'{[*visited, depends_on.absolute_path()]}'
                    )
                visited.append(depends_on.absolute_path())
        except KeyError as e:
>           warnings.warn(
                UserWarning(
                    f'depends_on chain {depends_on} references missing node {e}'.replace(
                        '\n', ''
                    )
                ),
                stacklevel=2,
            )

depends_on = DependsOn(parent='/entry/instrument/normalization_monitor', value='/entry/instrument/normalization_monitor/transformations/normalization_monitor_t0_r')

    def parse_depends_on_chain(
        parent: Field | Group, depends_on: DependsOn
    ) -> TransformationChain | None:
        """Follow a depends_on chain and return the transformations."""
        chain = TransformationChain(depends_on.parent, depends_on.value)
        # Use raw h5py objects to follow the chain because that avoids constructing
        # expensive intermediate snx.Group objects.
        file = parent.underlying.file
        visited = [depends_on.absolute_path()]
        try:
            while not depends_on.is_terminal:
                transform, base = _locate_depends_on_target(
                    file, depends_on, parent.definitions
                )
                depends_on = DependsOn(parent=base, value=transform.attrs['depends_on'])
                chain.transformations[transform.name] = transform[()]
                if depends_on.absolute_path() in visited:
                    raise ValueError(
                        'Circular depends_on chain detected: '
                        f'{[*visited, depends_on.absolute_path()]}'
                    )
                visited.append(depends_on.absolute_path())
        except KeyError as e:
>           warnings.warn(
                UserWarning(

                        '\n', ''

                stacklevel=2,

parent = <scippnexus.base.Group object at 0x7f0407a18ad0>
depends_on = DependsOn(parent='/entry/instrument/normalization_monitor', value='/entry/instrument/normalization_monitor/transformations/normalization_monitor_t0_r')

    def parse_depends_on_chain(
        parent: Field | Group, depends_on: DependsOn
    ) -> TransformationChain | None:
        """Follow a depends_on chain and return the transformations."""
        chain = TransformationChain(depends_on.parent, depends_on.value)
        # Use raw h5py objects to follow the chain because that avoids constructing
        # expensive intermediate snx.Group objects.
        file = parent.underlying.file
        visited = [depends_on.absolute_path()]
        try:
            while not depends_on.is_terminal:
                transform, base = _locate_depends_on_target(
                    file, depends_on, parent.definitions
                )
                depends_on = DependsOn(parent=base, value=transform.attrs['depends_on'])
                chain.transformations[transform.name] = transform[()]
                if depends_on.absolute_path() in visited:
                    raise ValueError(
                        'Circular depends_on chain detected: '
                        f'{[*visited, depends_on.absolute_path()]}'
                    )
                visited.append(depends_on.absolute_path())
        except KeyError as e:
>           warnings.warn(
                UserWarning(
                    f'depends_on chain {depends_on} references missing node {e}'.replace(
                        '\n', ''
                    )
                ),
                stacklevel=2,
            )

parent = <scippnexus.base.Group object at 0x7f3bc0105d90>
depends_on = DependsOn(parent='/entry/instrument/normalization_monitor', value='/entry/instrument/normalization_monitor/transformations/normalization_monitor_t0_r')

    def parse_depends_on_chain(
        parent: Field | Group, depends_on: DependsOn
    ) -> TransformationChain | None:
        """Follow a depends_on chain and return the transformations."""
        chain = TransformationChain(depends_on.parent, depends_on.value)
        # Use raw h5py objects to follow the chain because that avoids constructing
        # expensive intermediate snx.Group objects.
        file = parent.underlying.file
        visited = [depends_on.absolute_path()]
        try:
            while not depends_on.is_terminal:
                transform, base = _locate_depends_on_target(
                    file, depends_on, parent.definitions
                )
                depends_on = DependsOn(parent=base, value=transform.attrs['depends_on'])
                chain.transformations[transform.name] = transform[()]
                if depends_on.absolute_path() in visited:
                    raise ValueError(
                        'Circular depends_on chain detected: '
                        f'{[*visited, depends_on.absolute_path()]}'
                    )
                visited.append(depends_on.absolute_path())
        except KeyError as e:
>           warnings.warn(
                UserWarning(
                    f'depends_on chain {depends_on} references missing node {e}'.replace(
                        '\n', ''
                    )
                ),
                stacklevel=2,
            )

depends_on = DependsOn(parent='/entry/instrument/normalization_monitor', value='/entry/instrument/normalization_monitor/transformations/normalization_monitor_t0_r')

    def parse_depends_on_chain(
        parent: Field | Group, depends_on: DependsOn
    ) -> TransformationChain | None:
        """Follow a depends_on chain and return the transformations."""
        chain = TransformationChain(depends_on.parent, depends_on.value)
        # Use raw h5py objects to follow the chain because that avoids constructing
        # expensive intermediate snx.Group objects.
        file = parent.underlying.file
        visited = [depends_on.absolute_path()]
        try:
            while not depends_on.is_terminal:
                transform, base = _locate_depends_on_target(
                    file, depends_on, parent.definitions
                )
                depends_on = DependsOn(parent=base, value=transform.attrs['depends_on'])
                chain.transformations[transform.name] = transform[()]
                if depends_on.absolute_path() in visited:
                    raise ValueError(
                        'Circular depends_on chain detected: '
                        f'{[*visited, depends_on.absolute_path()]}'
                    )
                visited.append(depends_on.absolute_path())
        except KeyError as e:
>           warnings.warn(

                        '\n', ''

                stacklevel=2,

parent = <scippnexus.base.Group object at 0x7f3cf46a2b40>
depends_on = DependsOn(parent='/entry/instrument/normalization_monitor', value='/entry/instrument/normalization_monitor/transformations/normalization_monitor_t0_r')

    def parse_depends_on_chain(
        parent: Field | Group, depends_on: DependsOn
    ) -> TransformationChain | None:
        """Follow a depends_on chain and return the transformations."""
        chain = TransformationChain(depends_on.parent, depends_on.value)
        # Use raw h5py objects to follow the chain because that avoids constructing
        # expensive intermediate snx.Group objects.
        file = parent.underlying.file
        visited = [depends_on.absolute_path()]
        try:
            while not depends_on.is_terminal:
                transform, base = _locate_depends_on_target(
                    file, depends_on, parent.definitions
                )
                depends_on = DependsOn(parent=base, value=transform.attrs['depends_on'])
                chain.transformations[transform.name] = transform[()]
                if depends_on.absolute_path() in visited:
                    raise ValueError(
                        'Circular depends_on chain detected: '
                        f'{[*visited, depends_on.absolute_path()]}'
                    )
                visited.append(depends_on.absolute_path())
        except KeyError as e:
>           warnings.warn(
                UserWarning(
                    f'depends_on chain {depends_on} references missing node {e}'.replace(
                        '\n', ''
                    )
                ),
                stacklevel=2,
            )

parent = <scippnexus.base.Group object at 0x7f6238719010>
depends_on = DependsOn(parent='/entry/instrument/normalization_monitor', value='/entry/instrument/normalization_monitor/transformations/normalization_monitor_t0_r')

    def parse_depends_on_chain(
        parent: Field | Group, depends_on: DependsOn
    ) -> TransformationChain | None:
        """Follow a depends_on chain and return the transformations."""
        chain = TransformationChain(depends_on.parent, depends_on.value)
        # Use raw h5py objects to follow the chain because that avoids constructing
        # expensive intermediate snx.Group objects.
        file = parent.underlying.file
        visited = [depends_on.absolute_path()]
        try:
            while not depends_on.is_terminal:
                transform, base = _locate_depends_on_target(
                    file, depends_on, parent.definitions
                )
                depends_on = DependsOn(parent=base, value=transform.attrs['depends_on'])
                chain.transformations[transform.name] = transform[()]
                if depends_on.absolute_path() in visited:
                    raise ValueError(
                        'Circular depends_on chain detected: '
                        f'{[*visited, depends_on.absolute_path()]}'
                    )
                visited.append(depends_on.absolute_path())
        except KeyError as e:
>           warnings.warn(
                UserWarning(
                    f'depends_on chain {depends_on} references missing node {e}'.replace(
                        '\n', ''
                    )
                ),
                stacklevel=2,
            )

parent = <scippnexus.base.Group object at 0x7fed641e57c0>
depends_on = DependsOn(parent='/entry/instrument/normalization_monitor', value='/entry/instrument/normalization_monitor/transformations/normalization_monitor_t0_r')

    def parse_depends_on_chain(
        parent: Field | Group, depends_on: DependsOn
    ) -> TransformationChain | None:
        """Follow a depends_on chain and return the transformations."""
        chain = TransformationChain(depends_on.parent, depends_on.value)
        # Use raw h5py objects to follow the chain because that avoids constructing
        # expensive intermediate snx.Group objects.
        file = parent.underlying.file
        visited = [depends_on.absolute_path()]
        try:
            while not depends_on.is_terminal:
                transform, base = _locate_depends_on_target(
                    file, depends_on, parent.definitions
                )
                depends_on = DependsOn(parent=base, value=transform.attrs['depends_on'])
                chain.transformations[transform.name] = transform[()]
                if depends_on.absolute_path() in visited:
                    raise ValueError(
                        'Circular depends_on chain detected: '
                        f'{[*visited, depends_on.absolute_path()]}'
                    )
                visited.append(depends_on.absolute_path())
        except KeyError as e:
>           warnings.warn(
                UserWarning(
                    f'depends_on chain {depends_on} references missing node {e}'.replace(
                        '\n', ''
                    )
                ),
                stacklevel=2,
            )

    def parse_depends_on_chain(
        parent: Field | Group, depends_on: DependsOn
    ) -> TransformationChain | None:
        """Follow a depends_on chain and return the transformations."""
        chain = TransformationChain(depends_on.parent, depends_on.value)
        # Use raw h5py objects to follow the chain because that avoids constructing
        # expensive intermediate snx.Group objects.
        file = parent.underlying.file
        visited = [depends_on.absolute_path()]
        try:
            while not depends_on.is_terminal:
                transform, base = _locate_depends_on_target(
                    file, depends_on, parent.definitions
                )
                depends_on = DependsOn(parent=base, value=transform.attrs['depends_on'])
                chain.transformations[transform.name] = transform[()]
                if depends_on.absolute_path() in visited:
                    raise ValueError(
                        'Circular depends_on chain detected: '
                        f'{[*visited, depends_on.absolute_path()]}'
                    )
                visited.append(depends_on.absolute_path())
        except KeyError as e:
>           warnings.warn(

                        '\n', ''
                    )
                ),
                stacklevel=2,
            )
